| `#374151` | Body text, form labels, **section meta/info icons** (calendar · wrench · phone · wallet · map-pin · message — any glyph that LABELS a piece of data; must be clearly visible, sprint 496) |
| `#6b7280` | Muted text, hints, secondary captions |
| `#9ca3af` | Placeholders, faint **affordance-only** icons (search-box glyph, chevrons, spinners, empty-state illustrations), faint labels — **NOT data icons** (those are `#374151`) |
| `#cbd5e1` | Input border on hover |
| `#e5e7eb` | Default borders, dividers |
| `#d1d5db` | Disabled control fill (solid, see §2 buttons) |
| `#f3f4f6` | Muted background (ghost hover, disabled input) |
| `#fafafa` | Page background |
| `#ffffff` | Surface (cards) |

**Semantic**
| Meaning | Tokens |
|---|---|
| Success | `emerald-50` bg / `emerald-700` text / `emerald-200` border |
| Warning / caution | `amber-50` bg / `amber-700` text / `amber-200` border (deep text `#92400e`, fill `#fde68a`) |
| Error | `red-500` text, `red-400` input border, `red-50`/`red-700`/`red-200` badge |

> Note: `globals.css` also defines a legacy green `--color-primary-*` palette. The **live brand is the blue `#009FD9`** used by `Button`/`Badge`/`Input` — use the blue, not the green tokens.

### Typography

Font: **Inter** / system-ui sans (set globally). Scale (Tailwind):
| Role | Class | Notes |
|---|---|---|
| Page title | `text-2xl`/`text-3xl` `font-bold` `text-[#111827]` | One per page |
| Section heading | `text-lg`/`text-xl` `font-semibold` | |
| Card title / strong label | `text-sm`/`text-base` `font-semibold` | |
| Body | `text-sm` `text-[#374151]` | Default |
| Label (form) | `text-sm font-medium text-[#374151]` | |
| Hint / caption / meta | `text-xs` `text-[#6b7280]` | |
| Micro-label (eyebrow) | `text-[10px] font-semibold uppercase tracking-wide text-[#9ca3af]` | e.g. Mañana/Tarde/Noche |

Don't go below `text-xs` for anything the user must read (the `10px` eyebrow is for ALL-CAPS labels only). Numbers/prices: `font-semibold`, `whitespace-nowrap`.

### Spacing scale

Use the standard 4px Tailwind steps. Defaults for this app:
- **Page padding:** `py-6` (`px-4` mobile → container).
- **Between cards / major blocks:** `gap-4` / `space-y-4`.
- **Inside a card:** padding `p-4` (compact) — not `p-5`/`p-6`. Internal stacks `gap-3` / `space-y-3`.
- **Tight clusters** (label+control, chip rows): `gap-1.5` / `gap-2`.
- Prefer removing vertical space over adding it. If two blocks relate, keep them close.

### Radius & elevation
- Radius: cards/inputs/buttons `rounded-xl` (12px); chips/badges/pills `rounded-full`; small buttons `rounded-lg`. Big hero CTAs only `rounded-2xl`.
- **Elevation convention — the ONE rule for card/container shadows (apply everywhere):** there is exactly ONE card elevation, **`shadow-sm`**, and a container gets it **only if it visually FLOATS as a separate, scannable/tappable ITEM**. Concretely:
  - **Item cards → `shadow-sm`** (the **`Card` primitive**: `rounded-2xl border border-[#e5e7eb] bg-white shadow-sm`): a discrete record in a `gap`-separated list — a received **solicitud / proyecto / propuesta**, a **support ticket**, a **/buscar result**. These are the only shadowed containers.
  - **Everything else is FLAT** — `border border-[#e5e7eb]`, **no shadow**: **form/section containers** (Mi perfil, Servicios, Disponibilidad, Cuenta y seguridad, Verificación), **single row-list containers** (notificaciones, favoritos — ONE bordered box with `divide-y` rows, not separated cards), **info/empty-state boxes**, and the **panel shell/rail**.
  - Raised **primary buttons** + floating **popovers / drawers / modals** may also use `shadow-sm`. **Never** stack shadows or go heavier than `shadow-sm`. Borders (not shadows) carry structure; the lone `shadow-sm` only signals "this is a distinct item you act on."
- **No nested bordered containers (standing rule).** Never wrap a bordered card inside another bordered card inside a bordered section ("boxes within boxes") — it reads cluttered and dated. **At most ONE border level deep** in any region. Separate content with **spacing, a light divider (`border-t border-[#f3f4f6]`), or typographic hierarchy (heading + muted subtext)** — not another box. Concretely: a dashboard section heading sits **directly on the page** (`#fafafa`), not inside its own card; the section's body brings only the light containers it genuinely needs (e.g. individual list/feed items, each a single bordered card). Sub-blocks of a form (e.g. "Tus profesiones" / "Tus servicios") must be styled **consistently** — either both plain (divider-separated) or both a single subtle surface — never one boxed and the other not. Prefer a soft tint surface (`bg-[#f9fafb]`, no border) over a hard border when you need to group. Apply this to BOTH dashboards and the public profile.

### Components & standards

**Success-confirmation icon** — use the shared **`<SuccessIcon size={…} />`** (`components/ui/success-icon.tsx`) on EVERY "creado / enviado / completado" confirmation screen (support modal + public /soporte, /reset-password, booking success, /registro/cliente, leave-review + report-profile modals, …). It's the on-brand "check on a light tint of the same blue" badge: a pale brand-blue circle (`#EBF5FB`) + a subtle inset brand ring (`ring-[#009FD9]/20`) + a soft brand-blue glow + a bold `#009FD9` `Check`. **Do NOT** hand-roll a grey circle or a bare green check — always reuse `SuccessIcon` so all confirmations look identical (Sprint 158).

**"Check your spam" reminder** — on EVERY screen that tells the user to look in their email (OTP/verification, sign-up confirmation, password-recovery "email sent", email-change confirmation, support ticket created, the navbar login-modal "¡Revisa tu correo!"), drop the shared **`<SpamNotice className?/>`** (`components/ui/spam-notice.tsx`) directly UNDER the "check your email" line. It's subtle grey `text-xs text-[#9ca3af]` with ONE shared wording (`common.spamNotice`, es+en). Some providers (Outlook) spam our mail, so this prevents "it never arrived" confusion. **Do NOT** hand-write per-screen spam sentences (they drift) — always reuse `SpamNotice` (Sprint 201).

**"Revisa tu correo" screens get a RESEND option (Sprint 254).** Any "we sent you an email" screen (password-reset sent, email-change pending) shows, under the `SpamNotice`, a muted **"¿No te llegó el correo?"** + a **"Reenviar correo"** button backed by the shared **`useResendCooldown(seconds=30)`** hook: a live countdown disables it for a brief wait after each send (`"Reenviar en {n} s"`), then re-enables it; a successful resend shows **"Correo reenviado"**. Strings live in the **`common`** namespace (`resend`/`resendIn`/`resending`/`resent`/`resendPrompt`) so every screen is identical — don't re-roll a per-screen resend.

**Forgot-password is NEUTRAL — never reveal whether an email is registered (anti-enumeration).** The reset "sent" screen shows the SAME message for ANY email — **"Si hay una cuenta asociada a ese correo, te enviaremos un enlace…"** (NOT "Correo enviado", which would confirm existence). Supabase `resetPasswordForEmail` already returns success regardless of existence, so the email only actually sends when the account exists — but the UI copy must stay conditional. (Contrast: the LOGIN screen MAY be method-specific — "Esta cuenta usa Google" — because the user is proving they know the password; forgot-password has no such proof, so it must stay neutral.)

**Buttons** (`Button` component). Clear hierarchy, **compact sizing by default**:
- **Primary** — `variant="default"` (filled brand `#009FD9`). Exactly one per context. The main action ("Solicitar servicio", "Generar", "Guardar").
- **Secondary** — `variant="secondary"` (white, brand border/text) or `variant="outline"` (neutral). Supporting actions.
- **Ghost** — `variant="ghost"` for low-emphasis/tertiary (icon buttons, "Cancelar").
- **WhatsApp** — `variant="whatsapp"` only for the WhatsApp contact action.
- **Size:** default to **`size="sm"`** (`h-9 px-3.5 text-[13px]` — sprint 504; raised from the too-short `h-8 px-3 text-xs`/32px-12px so a secondary action is a comfortable **36px** tap target with readable text, while staying smaller than `md`) for in-card / dense contexts and **`size="md"`** (`h-10`/40px, `text-sm`) for form submits. Reserve `lg`/`xl` for the landing/hero. Compact is the rule, but **never below `sm` = h-9/36px** for a genuine tap target (full-width on mobile supplies the rest of the area).
- **Disabled state must READ as disabled, not just dimmed.** The default `disabled:opacity-50` makes a primary button look like a faded-but-clickable button. When a control is blocked by validation, render a **solid gray** state (`bg-[#d1d5db] text-white shadow-none hover:bg-[#d1d5db]`) + `aria-disabled`, so it's unmistakably inert. Pair it with **one** clear reason (inline), never a second duplicate message.

**Inputs** (`Input`/`Select`): `rounded-xl border border-[#e5e7eb] bg-white px-4 py-2.5 text-sm`, focus `ring-2 ring-[#009FD9]`, error `border-red-400`. Label above (`text-sm font-medium text-[#374151]`); hint/error below (`text-xs`). Always pair a label with its control; never rely on placeholder as the label. **Choice dropdowns use the shared popover, not a native `<select>` (sprint 283).** The canonical pattern is **`TimeSelect`** (Disponibilidad hours) and the generic **`SelectMenu`** (`components/ui/select-menu.tsx`): a button trigger (selected label, else muted placeholder) + a rotating `ChevronDown`, opening a listbox where the selected option is highlighted `bg-[#EBF5FB] text-[#0089bb]` with a `Check`; closes on outside-click/Escape, scrolls the selection into view. Use `SelectMenu` for provincia/cantón and any `{value,label}` choice (it backs `WorkplacesPicker` + `CoverageAreaSelector` + the **publicar-proyecto** modal as of sprint 311 — the last native-`<select>` holdout for provincia/cantón, now migrated) so every dropdown opens + reads the same — don't hand-roll a native select with an overlaid chevron. **For an OPTIONAL filter** (publicar proyecto's provincia/cantón), include a **first reset item** `{ value: "", label: "Todas/Todos" }` so the empty/default state is selectable (the placeholder isn't needed when a "Todas" option exists). **Trigger border (sprint 311):** states are mutually exclusive — **open** → `ring-2 ring-[#009FD9]` + `border-transparent`; **error** → `border-red-400`; otherwise neutral `border-[#e5e7eb]` that **darkens to `#cbd5e1` on hover** (the Input hover spec) so it reads as interactive. (Previously the neutral border overrode the open transparent border — fixed.) **Options popup (sprint 321):** `max-h-72` (≈8 rows) so a SHORT list shows in full (all 7 provinces, no awkward partial scroll) while a LONG list (cantones, DOB years/months) scrolls; it grows to the widest option (`w-max`, never narrower than the trigger, capped to the viewport) so option text is never cut off even when the trigger sits in a half-width cell. **Provincia/cantón are laid out SIDE-BY-SIDE (`grid grid-cols-2 gap-3`)** in BOTH the publicar-proyecto modal AND the `WorkplacesPicker` ("¿En qué zonas ofreces tus servicios?") so the two places match (sprint 321 un-did the sprint-301 full-width stack — the popup's grow-to-content makes the side-by-side safe from cut-off). **The popup is PORTALED to `<body>` (sprint 331, via `useAnchoredPosition`)** so it is NEVER clipped by an ancestor `overflow` — the publicar-proyecto modal's scrolling body + the pro-panel accordion used to "cut off" the open options. The portal stops `pointerdown` propagation so an option click inside a Radix Dialog (e.g. the booking modal's DOB picker) doesn't trip the dialog's pointer-down-outside and close it. The outside-click handler checks both the trigger root AND the portaled list.

**Chips / tags / pills**: `rounded-full px-2.5 py-0.5 text-xs font-medium`. Selectable chips (location/profession tabs): brand-tint when active (`bg-[#EBF5FB] text-[#009FD9] border border-[#bfdbfe]`), neutral when not (`bg-[#f3f4f6] text-[#6b7280] border border-[#e5e7eb]`). Overflow → a muted **"+N"** chip rather than wrapping endlessly.

**Cards** (`Card`): white surface, `border border-[#e5e7eb]`, **`rounded-2xl`**, `shadow-sm`, `p-4` — this is the **item-card** treatment (see the elevation convention in §2: only floating list items are shadowed; sections/row-lists/forms are FLAT). In a list, cards must be **uniform height** — bound variable content (cap lists, consolidate optional rows, use `min-h-[…]`) so a sparse card matches a rich one. See §3/§4.

**Panel list cards:** `Mis publicaciones` is the canonical activity-card pattern. `Mis solicitudes`, `Solicitudes recibidas`, `Oportunidades`, and `Mis propuestas` must use the same closed-card anatomy: white `rounded-2xl border-[#e5e7eb] shadow-sm`, `hover:-translate-y-0.5 hover:shadow-md`, `p-4 sm:p-5`, a `h-10 w-10 rounded-xl bg-[#EBF5FB] text-[#0089bb]` leading icon, navy bold title, stacked 13px metadata rows with grey icons, and the same circular chevron. Expanded panels use `border-t border-[#f3f4f6] bg-gradient-to-b from-[#fcfdff] to-white`. Simple expanded details are flat rows (`icon + eyebrow + value/text`) like `Mis publicaciones`; do not wrap them in extra white boxes/cards/rings. Keep section-specific actions/data, but do not invent alternate closed-card layouts for these four sections.

**Panel section icons:** use the same icon everywhere a section appears (panel nav, card lead icon, navbar account menu, notifications). Current canon: `Solicitudes recibidas` = `CalendarCheck`; `Mis solicitudes` = `CalendarClock`; `Oportunidades` / `Mis propuestas` = `Handshake`; `Casos de éxito` = `Award`; `Mis publicaciones` = `ClipboardList`. Avoid `Inbox`, `FolderOpen`, `Send`, or generic image icons for these sections unless the section purpose changes.

**Oportunidades / Mis propuestas field icons:** the section icon (`Send`) is only for the card lead / section identity. Inside opportunity/proposal metadata, use distinct field icons: budget/price = a compact `₡` glyph icon, service/category = `Wrench`, description/message = `FileText`, phone = `Phone`, location = `MapPin`, date/time = calendar/clock. Do not reuse the opportunity icon for description or service rows. In expanded `Mis propuestas`, the price label must be `Tu precio:` / `Your price:` while the value carries the `₡` amount, so the colón symbol is not repeated.

**Sprint 572 override for Oportunidades / Mis propuestas icons:** supersedes the older icon bullets above where they mention `Send`, a compact currency glyph, or `Coins` for both budget and price. `Oportunidades` / `Mis propuestas` use `Handshake` everywhere for section identity (panel nav, card lead, navbar account menu, notifications). Client budget rows use `Banknote` because they represent a project budget/range; tint it brand-blue when a concrete budget exists and muted grey for `A convenir` / `To be agreed`. Pro-owned price rows such as `Tu precio` in Mis propuestas keep `Coins`; the value itself is the only place the currency symbol appears.

**Sprint 550 metadata label rule:** dashboard activity-card metadata rows should not rely on icons alone. Use `icon + short label + value` for scan rows too (`Fecha:`, `Servicio:`, `Zona:`, `Presupuesto:`, `Publicado:`), with the label muted and the value readable. The icon helps recognition; the text label removes ambiguity for users who do not interpret icons quickly. Expanded detail rows keep the same idea with the label as the small uppercase eyebrow above the value.

**Mis publicaciones field icons:** the card lead icon remains `ClipboardList` because the object is a publication/request brief. Inside the card metadata, the service/category row must use `Wrench`, not `ClipboardList`; proposal count uses `Users`, location uses `MapPin`, description uses `FileText`, and publish date uses `CalendarDays`.

**Solicitudes recibidas field icons:** in received request cards, service/category uses `Wrench`; the client's note/description uses `FileText`; phone uses `Phone`; ID uses `IdCard`; appointment date uses `CalendarClock`; location uses `MapPin`. Do not reuse `Wrench` for the client note.

**Professional profile description is required:** the main profile `Descripción` / `Description` field (`bio`) is part of the "Completa tu perfil" required checklist. Do not label it optional in the profile editor; show it with the required `*`. Optional copy is only valid for the separate identity verification suggestion, business name, social links, extra languages, insurers, and other non-completion extras.

> **Public profile About prices/workplaces (sprint 580).** In Sobre m? / About, Precios/Prices use the `Banknote` icon, matching opportunity budget and proposal price rows in the professional panel. Service-level prices render vertically: service name as a muted uppercase subtitle/label, then the price underneath as the darker readable value. Separate multiple prices with whitespace only, not internal divider lines, and do not add captions such as ?Precios de referencia.? Lugares de trabajo / Workplaces must show only the administrative area (`Cant?n, Provincia`) chosen by the pro, deduplicated; do not show map pin names, exact addresses, or place labels from the map in this fact row.

> **Public profile About label/value hierarchy (sprint 570).** In Sobre m? / About facts, the small field title/label is muted grey and uppercase; the content/value is darker readable text (`#374151`, medium weight), not another navy/bold title. Reserve stronger navy/brand emphasis only for true anchors such as prices inside the value. This mirrors the dashboard field rule: label grey, user-facing value dark.

> **Public profile About verification row removed (sprint 569).** Sobre m? / About must not show a standalone Confianza/Trust row with Verificado/Verified. The verification state belongs in the profile header badge only; repeating it inside About adds clutter. Keep About focused on useful profile details: description, experience, languages, insurers, prices, and workplaces.

> **Public profile About contact row removed (sprint 568).** Sobre m? / About must not show a standalone Contacto row listing WhatsApp, Llamada, or Correo. Those options belong to the profile contact card/action area, not the About facts. Keep About focused on description, trust/experience, languages, insurers, prices, and workplaces.

**Public professional About tab:** do not repeat the professional name or professions inside `Sobre mí` / `About`; those already belong to the profile header. The About tab should read as one unified fact list. If `bio` exists, render it as the first flat row with a `FileText` icon, `Sobre mí` / `About` label, and the description text, followed by verification, experience, languages, prices, and workplaces.

**Mis solicitudes closed-card beneficiary hint:** when a booking is for someone else, do not show the generic "Para otra persona" line in the closed card. Show the useful compact form `Cita para: {name}` (fallback to "Otra persona") so the user understands the context without opening the card. The expanded detail keeps the fuller "La cita es para" label.

**Navigation chrome:** desktop navbar dropdowns, responsive drawer, and the panel mobile bottom nav need enough shadow to read as foreground containers. Active navigation is shown with a brand-blue rule: bottom rule on desktop navbar items, left rule inside mobile drawers/sheets, and top rule on the panel bottom nav. Use a three-line `Menu` icon for navigation overflow ("Mas"); reserve three-dot icons for contextual item actions.

**Badges** (`Badge`): status/labels. `default` brand-tint, `success`, `warning`, `error`, `muted`, `verified` (solid brand), `featured` (solid accent). Use `verified` for "Identidad verificada".

**Verification status — always show one of two unmistakable states (legal/veracity rule).** A professional is **never** shown without a verification indicator (ambiguity is the risk). Two states, visually distinct, on the /buscar card, the public profile, and the booking modal:
- **Verified** → green "Identidad verificada" (`bg-[#dcfce7] text-[#15803d]`, `ShieldCheck`).
- **Not verified** → amber "Identidad sin verificar" (`bg-[#fef3c7] border-[#fde68a] text-[#92400e]`, `ShieldAlert`). Never leave it blank.
Unverified professionals **are listed and bookable** (not hidden) but, when contacting/booking one, show an honest notice: "Este profesional aun no ha verificado su identidad. Coordina con cuidado." Wording **informs, never endorses** — ContrataCR only *displays* status and does not vouch for or guarantee any professional. The pro's own Verificacion tab uses a single top status card for the current state (verified, pending, under review, or rejected), with the canonical verified badge only in the verified state; action cards below carry the ID/manual-review flows. **The Verificacion tab's SINGLE verify action (sprint 332-333) is the "Verifica tu identidad con tu cedula" card** — enter/confirm an ID number (labelled **"Numero de identificacion"**, never "Numero de cedula (DIMEX/NITE tambien)") → it's stored + checked against the padron → a valid national cedula verifies INSTANTLY and overwrites the name from the padron (profiles + Auth), DIMEX/NITE → manual review. This ONE card covers every non-verified case (correct a cedula that didn't auto-verify, a sin-cedula/revoked pro adding one, a re-check); the old redundant "Verificar mi identidad ahora" re-run button was removed. (Rejected pros also keep the appeal box.) Only **rejected** profiles are hidden from search. **Generic identity fields use "identificacion" in labels/errors** because they accept cedula/DIMEX/NITE; reserve "cedula" for copy that specifically explains the Costa Rican national cedula/padron path. Duplicate/format errors for the ID input render directly under that input, not as a detached alert above/below the card.

**Toggles**: a switch with a short, **state-aware** label/description — the text describes what's true now and what the toggle does, and never contradicts itself across states. (e.g. private on → "tus horarios están ocultos…"; private off → "Activa para ocultar tus horarios…").

---

## 3. Layout patterns

### /buscar — full-width three-column directory (THE standard search layout)
This is the signature screen and is **full-width** — NOT a narrow `max-w-7xl` centered container (that wastes horizontal space next to a map). Wrap it in `mx-auto max-w-[1920px] px-4 sm:px-6 lg:px-8` so it fills the screen on laptops/desktops. Three columns, Hulihealth-style, responsive:
- **xl+ (≥1280):** `filters sidebar (w-64, sticky)` · `results list (flex-1)` · `map (xl:w-[38%], sticky, full viewport height)`.
- **lg–xl (1024–1279):** `results` · `map (lg:w-[40%])`; filters move behind a **"Filtros" slide-over drawer**.
- **<lg (mobile): a clean VERTICAL flow (Yelp-style), NOT a bottom-sheet/List-Map toggle.** One single column, in order: **(1)** the **search bar** (`MobileServiceSearch`) → **(2)** the **filters as a single horizontally-scrollable chip row** (`<SearchFilters variant="chips"/>` — Categoría/Provincia/Cantón/Ordenar/Aseguradora dropdown chips + "cerca de mí"/"Solo verificados" toggle chips; `overflow-x-auto`, `shrink-0`, never wraps) → **(3)** the **map at a FIXED height** (`h-[42vh]`, rounded+bordered) → **(4)** the **count** → **(5)** the stacked **card list**. The whole page scrolls normally; cards NEVER overlap the map. The SAME single `GoogleMapPanel` is repositioned via `lg:order-*` (mobile flow vs desktop column) — never a 2nd instance.
- `SearchResultsLayout` owns the three slots + drawer (takes a `filters` prop). `SearchFilters` is a **vertical sidebar** (stacked search, category [typeable+browsable], province/cantón, sort, aseguradora, geolocation, verified, clear). **Filters live ONLY in the sidebar — never inside the cards.**
- **Sort ("Ordenar por") — the standard set:** Mejor calificados (`rating`, default) · Precio (de menor a mayor) (`priceAsc`) · Disponibilidad inmediata (`availability` — soonest upcoming slot first) · Cercanía (`cercania` — requests geolocation when picked).
- **Default ranking — verified first.** Across every sort, **verified professionals rank above unverified** (a stable secondary pass after the chosen sort; featured within each group), so clients are steered to verified pros automatically and pros have a built-in incentive to verify. Unverified still appear, just lower. Keep the single **"Solo identidad verificada"** toggle for clients who want only verified — do **not** add any other verification filter.

### Result cards — content-driven height, compact, dense (two-zone)
A professional is always **ONE card** (`rounded-2xl bg-white border`, never split per location). Height is **content-driven with a floor** — `md:min-h-[190px]` (NOT a hard fixed height): a sparse card stays compact, a rich one (multi-location + multi-profession + many slots) grows. Content is padded `pl-10` so it clears the number badge. On mobile it stacks (`flex-col`). Two zones:
- **Identity zone (`flex-1`, left):** 52px avatar (initials on `#EBF5FB`, or photo) · **name row — the NAME has horizontal priority**: top line is name (`flex-1`) + the compact right-aligned WhatsApp/call icons only. The **verified mark** sits **inline beside the name on desktop** but **drops to a small line BELOW the name on mobile** (`hidden md:inline-flex` inline + `md:hidden` under the name) so the name never loses space to the badge. The name uses **`line-clamp-2 md:line-clamp-1`** — on mobile it **wraps to 2 lines rather than truncating with "…"** (prefer the full name); single line on desktop. Don't put the badge on the name's line on mobile. (See "Verified mark & tag hierarchy".) · **business name** line · a row of **soft profession tags** · a **rating row** (one `#ff9b32` star + value + "· N reseñas", or "Sin reseñas todavía") · **location** line (`MapPin`) + **coverage** line (`Truck`), each truncating · **"Ver casos de éxito (N)"** pinned to the bottom (`mt-auto`). No bio.
- **Action zone (`md:w-[232px]`, left border):** a **price header** ("Desde ₡14 000/hora" — `Desde` eyebrow + value, `pr-9 md:pr-10` clears the favorite), then the availability panel (below) with the **"Solicitar servicio"** CTA pinned to the bottom — **identical on every card** (same size/style); the WhatsApp/llamar secondary icons sit to its right (see Availability "Action row").

**Verified mark & tag hierarchy (trust ≠ category — don't make them look alike):**
- **Verified mark goes to the RIGHT of the NAME** (where the eye looks for a trust mark), as a **subtle icon + label, NOT a bordered pill**: verified → `ShieldCheck` `h-4` + "Verificado" in green `#16a34a`; unverified → `ShieldAlert` + "Sin verificar" in amber `#b45309`. `shrink-0` so the name truncates first.
- **Profession tags** are **soft, muted, few**: `rounded-full bg-[#f3f4f6] text-[#6b7280] px-2 py-0.5 text-[11px]` (NO heavy brand border), **capped at 2** + a `+N` overflow. They read as categories, clearly distinct from the green trust mark — avoid a wall of identical heavy-bordered pills. The **`+N` is a `<Link>` to the pro's profile** (where every profession is listed) — same pill shape, `relative z-10` (above the whole-card overlay), `hover:bg-[#EBF5FB] hover:text-[#009FD9]` + pointer so it reads as interactive, consistent with the card's other profile links (`card.moreProfessions` title/aria-label). Contact-only ("Coordina por WhatsApp"/"Solo WhatsApp", `Lock`) and `featured` are soft no-border tinted chips in the same row.
- Numbered cards **1..N** mirrored on numbered map pins; pin hover adds `ring-2 ring-[#009FD9]` + scrolls the card (map reaches it via `#pro-card-<id>`). Number badge top-left (`top-2.5 left-2.5`); a **small, subtle favorite** (`w-7 h-7`, white/subtle border, `Bookmark`) top-right (`top-2.5 right-2.5`) — consistent on every card. The name (`pr-9 md:pr-0`) and the price row (`pr-9 md:pr-10`) clear the favorite so it **never overlaps**, on desktop or mobile.
- Overflow always **truncates/collapses ("+N")**.
- **Icons:** keep them **small, few, and from ONE set** (lucide `h-3`–`h-4`, muted, mostly brand/`#9ca3af`) so they aid scanning without looking busy — the **only** exception is the official `WhatsAppIcon` for the WhatsApp action. Don't mix icon families or oversize them. **Pick the icon that REPRESENTS the thing, not a generic stand-in:** e.g. the account chooser ("¿Cómo quieres usar ContrataCR?", on `/registro` + `/onboarding`) uses **`UserRoundSearch`** for "Busco profesionales" (looking for a person) and **`BriefcaseBusiness`** for "Soy profesional" — not a bare `Search`/`Briefcase`. For role/choice cards the icon sits in a **polished brand tile that matches the `BrandIconBadge` treatment** (sprint 364): `h-16 w-16 rounded-full bg-[#EBF5FB]` + an **inset ring `ring-1 ring-inset ring-[#009FD9]/20`** + a **soft glow `shadow-[0_10px_30px_-10px_rgba(0,159,217,0.5)]`**, icon `h-8 #009FD9`. Keep the **hover fill** (`group-hover:bg-[#009FD9]` + icon `group-hover:text-white`, glow deepens) via `transition-all` — the ring+glow is what makes it read refined/on-brand (not a flat tint circle), so don't drop back to the plain `bg-[#EBF5FB]`-only tile.

### Availability display (Hulihealth-style)
- **Packed day columns** (3 at a time): show only upcoming days that **actually have bookable slots** (no "—" filler), with **uppercase** labels (`Hoy`/`Mañana`/`Jue 11`, brand-colored when soon ≤ tomorrow), ~2 time chips per day (brand-tint pills) + a **dashed `+N` chip**; chevrons page further out. Empty → one compact line ("Sin horarios próximos. Solicita el servicio para coordinar."). Slots are one-click → opens booking pre-selected.
- **Location selector** above the strip when the pro publishes hours at more than one place — **horizontal scrollable TABS** (`MapPin` + name; selected = brand-blue + underline), with the selected location's address shown directly below, defaulting to the first; single-location shows a `MapPin` + name (+ address) line. See §50 for the full rule (options from workplaces∪slots, strict per-id filter). Dedupe by id.
- Grouped lists (e.g. the pro's own "Tus horarios próximos") group times by **Mañana (<12h) / Tarde (12–18h) / Noche (≥18h)** so dense lists are scannable; each part renders only when it has slots.
- **Action button** (bottom of the availability panel): **"Solicitar servicio"** is a **single FULL-WIDTH primary** (`w-full`), identical on every card. The **direct-contact icons do NOT sit beside it** (that would vary card width / tempt a second row). Instead the **WhatsApp + call icon-buttons live in the card's TOP row, next to the name** (pushed right with `ml-auto`) — reusing existing horizontal space so **the card never grows taller**. They are **borderless** (no box/container): bare icons in a `h-7 w-7` tap target, `rounded-full`, with a **hover-only subtle circular highlight** (WhatsApp `text-[#1ebe5d] hover:bg-[#25D366]/10`; call `text-[#6b7280] hover:bg-[#EBF5FB] hover:text-[#009FD9]`). Official `WhatsAppIcon` + `Phone`, ~18px. They show only when enabled (WhatsApp when bookable + not appointments-only; call when `allowPhoneCall`); their presence/absence must not change card height or the button size. Contact-only (private / WhatsApp-only) cards show a **full-width "Solicitar por WhatsApp"** primary instead (the call icon, if any, is in the top row).
- **Verified wording:** the trust mark reads **"Identidad verificada"** (green `ShieldCheck`) on cards and profiles (unverified → amber `ShieldAlert` "Sin verificar"). Keep it a small icon+label, visually distinct from profession chips.
- **Category labels must never leak raw i18n keys:** if `tCat(id)` returns a `"categories.xxx"` path (missing message), fall back to `getCategoryLabel(id)` from the taxonomy (e.g. `otro` → "Otro servicio"). The `otro` key is now in `messages/{es,en}.json`.
- **The app is now fully bilingual (ES/EN).** Every user-facing surface — landing/standalone pages, search, BOTH dashboards and all their sections, account/support, and all auth/flow pages (registro, onboarding, completar-perfil, reset/forgot password, publicar-proyecto, notificaciones, proveedores-autorizados, contacto) — uses `useTranslations`/`getTranslations` with paired `messages/{es,en}.json` namespaces. When adding ANY new copy, add both ES + EN keys; never hardcode Spanish (or English) strings in a component. Locale-aware dates use `locale === "en" ? "en-US" : "es-CR"`; taxonomy labels use `getCategoryLabel(id, locale)`. Intentionally untranslated (Spanish only, by design): `/terminos`, `/privacidad` (binding legal — human review), and the entire `admin/*` panel.
- **Private / contact-only:** shown as a compact **inline chip** in the card's chips row ("🔒 Coordina por WhatsApp" / "Solo WhatsApp") — NOT a full-width band — so it never adds card height; the availability column then renders just the compact WhatsApp/llamar actions (pinned to the bottom).
- **Insurance (aseguradora) filter:** **no "Todas" option.** Default is **NONE selected** (placeholder "Cualquiera", no filtering → normal results). Picking an insurer filters to pros who accept it. **Clearing is an in-dropdown "Cualquier aseguradora" reset item** (shown only when one is picked, mapped from a `__any__` sentinel since Radix forbids an empty value) — **NOT** an external X button beside the select. The old X-beside pattern shrank this one control ~40px narrower than the other sidebar filters; keeping the clear inside the dropdown means **every /buscar filter field is the exact same full-width `h-10` size** (uniform-filter-size rule, sprint 305). Same pattern for any "optional single-select filter that defaults to off".
- **All /buscar sidebar/drawer filter fields are ONE uniform size AND the SAME shape (sprint 314).** Every filter — the unified **Servicio** search/category field + Provincia + Cantón + Ordenar por + Aseguradora — is now structurally identical: a **`fieldLabel`** (`mb-1 text-[11px] font-semibold text-[#6b7280]`) above an **`h-10 w-full rounded-xl border border-[#e5e7eb] bg-white px-4 text-sm`** box, ALL inside the one `flex flex-col gap-3` group. Matching `h-10 w-full` alone was NOT enough — the service field still LOOKED different because it (a) had **no label** while the four dropdowns had labels, (b) used **`pl-9 pr-9`** padding for a left search icon (so its text started ~36px in vs the dropdowns' 16px `px-4`), and (c) lived in a separate `flex` wrapper above the group. Fix: gave it a **"Servicio"** label, **dropped the left search icon** so it uses **`pl-4`** (text starts at the same x as the dropdowns), moved it INTO the group as the first field, and gave it a **right-side glyph that mirrors the dropdown chevron EXACTLY** — `absolute right-4 top-1/2 -translate-y-1/2 h-4 w-4 text-[#6b7280]`: a `Search` icon at rest, the clear-X while typing — with `pr-9` ALWAYS so the right padding matches the dropdowns' reserved chevron space (sprint 315). So now all five share the same box AND the same right affordance, perfectly aligned — match this when adding any filter. No field may carry an inline side control (e.g. a clear X) that changes its width. (The mobile `chips` variant is a separate horizontally-scrollable pill row, currently unused on /buscar.) **Clear-X vs close-X are visually distinct (sprint 327):** the /buscar filters have two X's that must never be confused — the **"clear my search" X** is a **tiny, subtle icon INSIDE the search field** (`h-3.5` in a hover-only `h-5` circle at `right-3`, only while there's a query), while the **"close the filters panel" X** (mobile drawer header) is a **distinct, larger FILLED circle button** (`h-8 w-8 rounded-full bg-[#f3f4f6]`, `X h-[18px]`, `active:scale-95`). Clearing filters entirely is a **LABELLED text link "Limpiar filtros (N)"** (brand-blue `text-[#009FD9]`, NO X icon — sprint 333 dropped the old bare-X so it can't read as a close). Keep these three affordances distinct (in-field clear = quiet/small X inside the field; **clear-all = a labelled "Limpiar filtros" text link**; close-panel = a prominent filled-circle X). The modern rule: a destructive-ish "clear everything" is a **labelled action**, never a bare ambiguous X. **Open menus match the FIELD width (sprint 322):** the four Radix `Select` filters (Provincia/Cantón/Ordenar/Aseguradora) open a menu pinned to the trigger's width and flush with it — `SelectContent className="min-w-0 w-[var(--radix-select-trigger-width)]"` (`--radix-select-trigger-width` is exposed on popper content; `min-w-0` drops the shared `min-w-[8rem]`) — so a short option list (e.g. Aseguradora) no longer opens narrower/left-misaligned than its full-width field. This mirrors the "Servicio" `AnchoredDropdown`, which already matches its input width. Scoped to the filters via `FILTER_CONTENT`; the shared `Select`/`SelectContent` (used elsewhere, where a content-width menu is fine) is untouched. The option list, the active-option `Check`, radius + shadow are unchanged.

### Client identity at booking (cédula) + masked display
- **Client SIGNUP cédula policy (updated sprint 336).** The STANDALONE browse-time signup (`registro/cliente`) still asks for **no cédula** — a client who only browses may never need one (cédula collected later at booking / `completar-perfil`). BUT the **booking-context registration** (the `ClientRegistrationModal` that opens when a guest taps "Solicitar servicio") is now **cédula-FIRST**: identity step (cédula → official name auto-fills from the padrón, or a "No tengo cédula" manual name) → email → password → OTP. The cédula is stored on the new account, so the booking that follows **does NOT ask for it again** (no double-ID). Rationale: a guest at "Solicitar servicio" is about to book, so the cédula is needed anyway — asking it once, up front, cédula-first, matches the logged-in booking flow. Reuses the shared `IdentityField` (cédula→padrón→name) + the brand-blue "No tengo cédula" escape. (Pros still provide a cédula at signup.) Do NOT re-add a cédula to the standalone browse signup.
- **Cédula is collected at booking, not signup.** A national cédula is confirmed against the **padrón**; the **official name prevails**. **For myself**: if the cédula's official name **differs** from the account name, show a **responsive amber warning** ("La cédula ingresada pertenece a **[Nombre oficial]**. Al confirmar, tu cuenta usará este nombre oficial. Usa únicamente tu propia cédula.") — contained box, `ShieldAlert`, `text-xs`, `break-words`, fits ~360px → desktop; on confirm the cédula links to the account, the name becomes official, and it **persists + prefills** future bookings. If the name **matches**, link silently. **For another person**: the beneficiary cédula is used **only on that booking** — **never** linked to the account, never changes the holder's name. **Early feedback (sprint 280):** an invalid/non-existent national cédula surfaces **"No encontramos esa cédula. Revisa el número e intenta de nuevo."** as soon as the field is COMPLETE (the debounced live padrón lookup), not only at submit — so the client isn't made to fill the whole form first; it never overrides the higher-priority "ya está registrada en otra cuenta" message, and DIMEX/NITE never trigger "not found" (not in the padrón by design). **No redundant sub-hint** under the field (the old "Solo para tu solicitud — no es una verificación de identidad." line was removed). **No-cédula + DIMEX (sprint 282):** the cédula is **not strictly required** — a **"No tengo cédula"** disclosure link lets the client proceed WITHOUT one (types their name manually); a **DIMEX/NITE** is accepted but flagged with an amber "no se verifica contra el padrón" note. A **national cédula** (padrón-confirmed) = **verified**; **DIMEX / no cédula** = **unverified** with a manually-typed name. **(Sprint 330)** the pro's received-request card NO LONGER shows a "Identidad verificada"/"Sin verificar" label (it was clutter): it just shows the **cédula number when provided, else a muted "Sin cédula"** — for both the requester and the beneficiary. (The verified state still gates ranking/badges elsewhere; the received-request card simply doesn't annotate it.) **Identification-FIRST, name auto-fills below (sprint 299):** in solicitar servicio (self AND the other person) the **identification field comes FIRST** and the **"Nombre completo" sits BELOW it** — a national cédula auto-fills the official name **read-only** (green box), while no-cédula / DIMEX shows a **manual** name input; never put the name above the ID. The **"for another person"** flow does NOT ask "¿La persona tiene cédula?" — it opens with a **"Datos de la otra persona"** section title and **simple labels below it** (**"Número de identificación"**, **"Nombre completo"**, **"Teléfono"**) — NOT "… de la otra persona" repeated on every field (sprint 310). It leads with the identification (the name auto-fills below) and offers a **"No tengo su cédula"** escape (manual name, flagged unverified to the pro), mirroring the self **"No tengo cédula"** path (both links brand-blue `#009FD9`). The escape no longer shows a verbose "Enviarás la solicitud sin cédula verificada de la persona…" notice (sprint 310) — it's form clutter, since the pro already sees the unverified status on the received request; only the toggle-back link ("Tengo su cédula"/"No tengo su cédula") remains. (NOTE: this beneficiary sub-block is currently hardcoded Spanish — a pre-existing EN-parity gap to fix when the booking flow's strings are migrated to i18n.) DIMEX is recognized + unverified for both. The booking only stores a beneficiary cédula when padrón-confirmed or DIMEX. (As of sprint 330 the pro's received-request card shows the beneficiary's **cédula if provided, else "Sin cédula"** — no verified/unverified label.)
- **Name lock follows a NATIONAL cédula — identically for clients AND professionals (sprint 316).** Whenever a **national** cédula (padrón-confirmed) is on file, the **Nombre completo is read-only** (lock icon + the `nameLockedHelp` message; corrections → soporte). **No cédula / DIMEX-NITE → name freely editable.** Both panels do this the SAME way: the **client** locks on `cedulaVerified` (`detectIdType(profiles.cedula) === "cedula"`); the **professional** locks on **`verification_status === "verified"` OR a national cédula** (the pro editor fetches the cédula via the owner-only `get_my_profile` RPC, since `cedula` isn't directly selectable — a pro who entered a national cédula at signup gets the locked name even before admin review finishes). The **`nameLockedHelp`** wording is parallel but context-accurate about WHERE the cédula was entered: client = "…la cédula que ingresaste en tu última solicitud de servicio…"; pro = "…la cédula que ingresaste **al crear la cuenta**…" (both end "¿Un error o cambio legal? Solicítalo a soporte."). The **"Verificado" badge appears ONCE** in the pro panel — in the **header** (next to the avatar + name, `Badge variant="verified"`), NOT also in the editor's "Nombre completo" field label (sprint 323 removed that duplicate). The locked name field shows just the **Lock icon + the `nameLockedHelp` text**, which already convey it's official + read-only; the header carries the trust badge.
- **Masked cédula display:** anywhere a saved cédula is shown back, mask it with `maskId()` → **last 4 digits only** (`X-XXXX-1234`; NITE/DIMEX `XXXXXX1234`). The client panel's **Identidad** card shows the masked cédula + a status chip (verified green / "Pendiente de revisión" amber for DIMEX/NITE).
- **The "¿Para quién? Para mí / Para otra persona" toggle is HEALTH-ONLY (sprint 366).** It shows **only when the effective booked category is `es_salud`** (`proIsHealth`); for every other category (place/object/deliverable AND non-health *person* services like beauty/fitness/classes) it's **hidden → requester-only**, and "it's for my kid/parent" just goes in the free-text description. Rationale (connector test): only collect beneficiary data when it changes what the pro does **before contact** — for a doctor/therapist/carer the patient's **age** is genuine clinical triage; for a haircut or guitar lesson the pro learns who shows up on contact. "Para otra persona" is explicitly for a **DEPENDENT** (a child / older / disabled relative who can't use the app), shown with a subtle helper line. Wording is care-aware (`isCareCategory()` = the 3 `cuidado_*` ids): **clinical → "¿Para quién es la cita?" + block "Datos del paciente"; care → "¿Para quién es el servicio?" + block "Datos de la persona"** (a niñera's child is not a "patient"). All strings live under `booking.forWho.*` (es+en).
- **A dependent (health) booking collects ONLY the beneficiary's NAME + DOB (sprint 366) — no cédula, no phone.** The beneficiary **cédula was cut** (the pro doesn't need the patient's ID to accept/prepare; it only auto-filled the name — not worth the padrón/DIMEX complexity) and the beneficiary **phone was cut** (the **requester** is the sole coordination contact, so the requester's phone is required regardless of who the service is for). The beneficiary name is a plain manual field; the DOB uses the shared `DateOfBirthPicker` and is **required**.
- **Health (es_salud) bookings require the patient's DOB** — for **myself** (manual input; the padrón has no birth date) and for **another person** (beneficiary DOB, not optional). Block the step's Continue/Submit until it's provided. Non-health bookings never ask DOB (data minimization). **The DOB control is the shared `DateOfBirthPicker`** (`components/ui/date-of-birth-picker.tsx`, sprint 318) — **three `SelectMenu` dropdowns in `Día · Mes · Año` order (dd/mm/yyyy, the CR convention)**, NOT a native `<input type="date">`: the YEAR is a plain scrollable dropdown (maxYear→1915) so picking a birth year is one scroll, never paging a calendar month-by-month; the day list clamps to the chosen month (31→Feb = 28/29). It emits/accepts an ISO `YYYY-MM-DD` so storage is unchanged. Any saved/locked DOB is shown via **`formatDobDMY(iso)` → `dd/mm/yyyy`** (never the raw ISO). Month names + placeholders are locale-aware (es/en). Reuse this picker for any date-of-birth field; keep native `<input type="date">` only for near-term calendar dates (e.g. the pro's blocked-dates).
- **Age-bracket marking is MINOR *and* ADULTO MAYOR, professional-only, derived from the DOB (Sprint 309).** Since health bookings already collect the patient's DOB, the pro's received request shows a small **age-bracket badge** — **"Menor de edad"** (under 18, amber `text-[#b45309] bg-[#fef3c7]` — signals guardian/consent) or **"Adulto mayor"** (65+, "adulto mayor" per CR Ley 7935; neutral grey `text-[#6b7280] bg-[#f3f4f6]` — informational/geriatric) — for **both** the self patient (from `client_dob`) and the beneficiary (from `beneficiary_dob`). A typical adult (18–64) shows nothing. It is genuinely useful clinical context for a medical professional, so we mark BOTH brackets consistently (the old "minor only" was incomplete). The bracket is **derived at display time** via `ageCategoryFromDob()` (`lib/age.ts`) — **no new column**; the legacy `beneficiary_is_minor` flag is kept only as a fallback for bookings stored before a DOB was retained. **Shown ONLY to the professional** (the `BookingRequests` panel), never as client-facing clutter (the client's own DOB note was removed in sprint 302). Keys `bookingRequests.minor` / `bookingRequests.olderAdult` (es+en).
- **No double-booking:** the booking API rejects (409) a slot already held by an active booking; the modal surfaces it inline ("Ese horario acaba de ser reservado…"). Cancelling/completing frees the slot again.

### Status filter tabs — no "Todas/Todos"
- **A section's primary action goes on its OWN row, never inline with the filter pills.** A "+ Publicar una solicitud" / "+ Contactar soporte" type button must NOT share a `justify-between` row with `StatusFilterTabs` — the four pills wrap to two lines at ~360px and shove the button to an awkward spot. Put the button on its own row **above** the tabs (`flex justify-end`, button `w-full sm:w-auto` = full-width primary on mobile, right-aligned on desktop), with the tabs on the line below (the soporte pattern). Order: heading → primary action → filter tabs → list.
- The shared `SOLICITUD_TABS` / `PROYECTO_TABS` (in `status-filter-tabs.tsx`) have **no all-bucket** — the status tabs already cover every lifecycle state, so "Todas/Todos" only added noise. **Both use the SAME three-bucket scheme** so the two panels read identically. **Default to the ACTIVE tab**: solicitudes → **"Activas"**; proyectos → **"Activos"**.
- Canonical labels (must match the real lifecycle): **solicitudes** = Activas (`pending/confirmed/in_progress/awaiting_confirmation`) · Finalizadas (`completed`) · Canceladas (`cancelled/rescheduled`). **proyectos** = Activos (`open/in_progress/awaiting_confirmation`) · Finalizados (`completed`) · Cancelados (`cancelled`). The finer open-vs-assigned distinction stays visible on each card's status **badge** ("Abierto" vs "En curso · Asignado") rather than as a separate confusing tab.
- **Soporte (tickets) uses the SAME shared `StatusFilterTabs` as solicitudes/proyectos** — tabs **Pendiente / En proceso / Resuelto** (`open` / `in_progress` / `resolved`) via `labelFor` (custom labels, since soporte's ids aren't the `statusTabs` keys), **no "Todas"**, **defaults to "Pendiente"**. Each tab shows its **per-status count** (the same brand-blue/white count badge) plus a small **red dot** (`dotFor`) when that status has an UNREAD reply. **No per-card status pill** — soporte tabs are 1:1 with status, so a status label on every card just repeats the tab (always redundant); removed it. A filtered-but-empty status shows the `noneInView` line.
- **Profession filters omit "Todas/Todos" too (Oportunidades sprints 478→489, Profesiones 486/492, Casos de éxito 493→495).** A per-profession filter (the pill/chip row, shown only when the pro has **2+ professions**) lists **only the pro's actual professions** — one chip each (`getCategoryLabel`), with a count — and has **no "Todas/Todos" option**. It **defaults to the FIRST profession** and the view ALWAYS filters to the active one (`items.filter(i => i.profession === activeProf)`); a guard (`useEffect` / derived value) re-points the active profession to a valid one if the pro's professions change, so nothing silently disappears. A single-profession pro sees no filter at all. This now includes the **CLIENT-facing case showcase** too (`case-showcase.tsx`, public profile — sprint 502 DROPPED the earlier "Todos" default per owner request): it lists only the pro's professions that have cases, **defaults to the first**, and shows **no filter when there's only one profession** — same rule as the owner panels (the brief sprint-497 "showcase keeps Todos" exception is retired).

### "Mis propuestas" (a pro's SENT proposals) — statuses + actions (sprint 449)
- **Same unified collapsible-accordion card as the other panel sections** (Solicitudes recibidas · Oportunidades · Mis solicitudes · Mis publicaciones): client avatar + project **title** (primary, `text-[15px] font-semibold truncate`) + a status chip on the right; key fact = **"Tu precio: ₡X"** (or "Precio a convenir"), led by a **`Coins`** icon (sprint 500 — the app's money/price glyph, used for BOTH "Tu precio" and a project's "Presupuesto"; NOT a `$`-based icon since CR uses ₡; dark `#374151`); a `line-clamp-2 [overflow-wrap:anywhere]` **message preview while collapsed**; expand → the full message (`whitespace-pre-line [overflow-wrap:anywhere]`), "Enviada el {fecha}", the client phone (once accepted), and the actions. Do NOT leave it as the old flat `CardContent` card.
- **The "waiting" badge here = "Pendiente"** (ES; "Pending" EN — sprint 499, clearer than the older terse "En espera"): a SENT propuesta genuinely awaits the client's decision, so the **amber "Pendiente"** badge shows in **Activas** (a *booking*'s `pending` stays **"Pendiente"** — it must read distinctly from the booking's later **"Esperando confirmación"**, so the two confirmation moments never blur). Accepted proposals overlay the PROJECT lifecycle (`projStatus`: Asignado · En curso / Trabajo realizado · esperando confirmación / Finalizado / Cancelado por el cliente). `proposalStatusRedundant` hides a badge only when it just repeats the active filter (accepted+in_progress in Activas, declined in Canceladas, completed in Finalizadas).
- **Actions = DIRECT buttons, NO "···" overflow menu (sprint 498).** Mis propuestas' states have at most two actions, and a lone action must never hide behind a menu — surface them directly: *pending* → **"Editar propuesta"** (outline) + **"Retirar propuesta"** (red-outline `border-[#fecaca] text-[#dc2626]`, destructive → a clean on-brand confirm modal, NEVER `window.confirm`); *accepted + active* → **"Contactar cliente"** (WhatsApp) + **"Marcar completado"** (outline, while in progress); *finalizada / rechazada / cliente-canceló* → no actions (status only). (`CardActionsMenu` is for sections that genuinely have 3+ actions; Mis propuestas no longer imports it.) **Retiring/editing a proposal MUST persist via the service-role admin client** — `proposals` has no UPDATE/DELETE RLS policy for the professional, so an RLS-bound `delete()`/`update()` silently affects **0 rows** and the change is lost (the "Retirar no guarda" bug, sprint 498); verify ownership + `status==='pending'` first, then mutate via `createAdminClient()`.
- **Oportunidades (browse) = MASTER–DETAIL (sprint 478, owner reference image).** Desktop `lg:grid lg:grid-cols-[minmax(0,1fr)_minmax(0,1.35fr)]`: compact opportunity LIST left + selected DETAIL pane right (`lg:sticky lg:top-4`); MOBILE collapses to one column where tapping a card expands the SAME detail inline (`lg:hidden` accordion). Selection = `selectedId` (default first; active card gets brand-blue border/tint/left-accent). **ICONS rule = GREY functional + BRAND-BLUE accents:** detail meta `Coins`(money — sprint 500, was `Wallet`)/`CalendarClock`/`MapPin`/`Clock` dark slate `#374151` (sprint 496 — was faint `#9ca3af`, too low-contrast); brand-blue ONLY on the callout `FileSearch` + the Consejo `Lightbulb`; dismiss `EyeOff` grey; LIST cards stay icon-free. **List card:** avatar + navy title (NO profession) + a **"Nueva"** green badge when posted TODAY + `zona · plazo` + **budget `#0089bb`** + `relativeTime` ("hace 1 h / 3 días / 2 semanas / 1 mes"); header "Oportunidades disponibles · N nuevas". **Detail (`renderDetail`, shared by pane + accordion):** Nueva + timeline badges + navy title + `MapPin zona · Clock Publicada {rel}`; brand-tint callout; "Descripción del proyecto"; budget·fecha meta; "Información del cliente" = avatar + name ONLY (we don't store client verified/projects/join or a remote/in-person preference — never fake them); then the proposal FORM (Consejo tip + price [no placeholder] + message [label "Tu propuesta", no placeholder] + Enviar + "Es gratis…" + "No me interesa"). **Filter by profession** only when `professions.length > 1`. **Already-proposed opportunities are filtered OUT** (they're in Mis propuestas) via the `submitted` Set (project_ids from `mine=true`, recomputed on every switch to browse). **Retiring a proposal FREES the opportunity (sprint 498):** `confirmWithdraw` removes the project_id from `submitted` (and the deleted proposal won't return from `mine=true`), so the project **reappears in Oportunidades** and can be re-proposed — consistent with the hide-already-proposed rule. **"No me interesa"** dismiss persists per-browser in `localStorage` (`cc_opps_dismissed`). "Mis propuestas" stays the accordion (unchanged).
- **WhatsApp after "Solicitar servicio" lives at BOTH moments (connector model).** The booking **success screen** shows WhatsApp as the full-width primary CTA ("Continuar por WhatsApp") for immediate contact while the client is motivated, AND **"Mis solicitudes"** shows WhatsApp "Contactar" as the primary on every ACTIVE booking for later access. This is intentional access at two moments, not harmful duplication — the connector model favors fast contact. Do NOT restrict contact to "only the professional reaches out" (slower for a motivated client).

### Filter tabs = underline + count badges (sprint 479, owner image)
- **`StatusFilterTabs` `variant="underline"` (default)** is the canonical status filter everywhere (Solicitudes recibidas · Mis solicitudes · Mis publicaciones · Mis propuestas · Soporte): tabs share ONE bottom hairline (`border-b border-[#eef0f2]`, each tab `-mb-px border-b-2`); ACTIVE = brand-blue underline + `text-[#009FD9]` + a **filled blue count pill** (`bg-[#009FD9] text-white`); inactive = `text-[#6b7280]` + a **light-grey count pill** (`bg-[#f3f4f6]`). The count comes from `counts[id]` and shows only when > 0 (`tabular-nums`, `min-w-[1.25rem]`). This REPLACES the sprint-419 "no counts" pills for status filters — the owner asked for per-filter counters with a reference image. **The SERVICE/category filters use the SAME underline+counts design (sprint 510):** **Servicios** (panel, count = precios per servicio), **Oportunidades** (count = available opps per service), and **Casos de éxito** (panel + public profile, count = casos per service) all pass `counts` to `variant="underline"` — `variant="pills"` is now effectively unused (one cohesive filter look app-wide). `dotFor` (attention dot) still works in both variants.

### Request/list cards with actions (e.g. "Solicitudes recibidas")
- **Vertical hierarchy, not a left/right split:** header (status badge + date) → details → a **footer separated by `border-top`** holding the actions. Never put the action buttons in a `shrink-0` right column — it cramps them on mobile.
- **Actions are full-width STACKED on mobile, inline-wrap on desktop:** wrapper `flex flex-col sm:flex-row sm:flex-wrap gap-2`, each button `className="w-full sm:w-auto"`. Keep the **same footer shape across every status** so cards stay uniform. Subtle/destructive links (e.g. "Reportar cliente") go last, `self-start`.
- **`BookingRequests` card matches the pixel-exact prototype (sprint 343).** **Filter tabs** = `Pendientes · Confirmadas · Finalizadas · Canceladas` (that order — NEVER "Activas", it merged pendientes+confirmadas). **The count is a bold, color-contrasted NUMBER inline after the label — NOT a circular badge (sprint 368).** A single digit can't be optical-centered inside a ~20px circle (the font baseline sits it ~0.5px high), and it kept reading as off across two attempts; the container was dropped. The number now sits on the **same text baseline as the label** (aligned by definition), `text-[13px] font-bold tabular-nums leading-none`, **brand-blue `text-[#009FD9]`** on the inactive white pill and **white** on the active blue pill. Position: **next to the word** (these are horizontal text pills; a floating-above badge would break the pill shape and cramp the 4-up row at ~360px). Shown **only when count > 0** (a present number means "there's something here"; empty tabs stay clean). Do NOT reintroduce a rounded-pill/circle container for the count. Tabs are pill buttons: active `bg-[#009FD9] text-white`, inactive `bg-white border-[#e5e7eb]`. **SOLICITUDES and PROYECTOS share this EXACT tab set + count badges (consistency)** — one source of truth in `status-filter-tabs.tsx`: `solicitudBucket(status, scheduledDate)` for bookings (a CONFIRMED appointment whose date passed auto-falls to **Finalizadas**), `proyectoBucket(status)` for a client's published project (open→Pendientes, in_progress/awaiting→Confirmadas, completed→Finalizadas, cancelled→Canceladas), and `proposalBucket(proposalStatus, projectStatus)` for a pro's proposal (pending→Pendientes, accepted+active→Confirmadas, accepted+completed→Finalizadas, declined/project-cancelled→Canceladas); `bucketCounts()` builds the per-tab map. Used by the pro panel (Solicitudes recibidas + Proyectos/Mis propuestas) AND the client panel (Mis solicitudes + Mis proyectos), all defaulting to **Pendientes**. **Don't repeat the active tab's status on each card.** Since the view is always inside a status tab (no all/mixed view), a card whose status just RE-states the tab (e.g. a "Confirmada" pill inside the Confirmadas tab) is redundant — hide it. Show the status pill ONLY for a genuine SUB-state that adds info within the bucket (e.g. "En progreso"/"Esperando confirmación" inside Confirmadas, "Reprogramada" inside Canceladas, a client-cancelled project among declined proposals). Helpers `solicitudStatusRedundant` / `proyectoStatusRedundant` / `proposalStatusRedundant` encode "is this the bucket's primary status?" When the badge is hidden in a card header, drop the now-empty left slot (`justify-end` so the date stays put). **This no-redundant-status rule is consistent across SOLICITUDES, PROYECTOS, AND SOPORTE.** Soporte is the simplest case — its tabs are 1:1 with status, so a per-card status pill ALWAYS repeats the active tab → it was removed outright (no helper needed). (If a mixed/"all" view is ever added, re-show the status there, where it distinguishes items.) **Card** is a `rounded-[18px] overflow-hidden` shell with FULL-WIDTH sections (each its own `px-[18px]` padding). **Keep dividers to the essentials (sprint 362): exactly TWO lines** — the people LEFT/RIGHT split and the actions-footer `border-t` — and let **spacing + eyebrow labels + type hierarchy** separate everything else (the old card stacked 5 lines and read "chopped up"). Do NOT add hairlines between the status header, the appointment, and the meta rows. Top→bottom: **(1) status header** (`pt-4 pb-1`, NO border — whitespace sets it off) — status pill left (Pendiente=amber `warning`, Confirmada/En progreso=blue `default`, Finalizada/Esperando=green `success`, Cancelada=red `error`, Reprogramada=grey `muted`; override `px-3 py-1 text-xs font-bold`) + muted **"Solicitada {date}"** right (the REQUEST date). **(2) FEATURED appointment date** — NO container/tile (clean): a **`CalendarClock` `h-[22px]`** icon to the LEFT in **gray `text-[#9ca3af]`** (matches the other meta icons — MapPin/FileText/Phone/IdCard — same color, NOT a different/brand color), beside the eyebrow **"FECHA DE LA CITA"** (`text-[10px] font-bold uppercase tracking-[0.06em] text-[#9ca3af]`) + the date `text-[16px] font-bold` formatted **"Mar, 23 jun · 1:00 pm"** (capitalised weekday, 12-hour time). **This is the canonical DATE treatment app-wide** — a gray calendar icon + the date, never a grey/tint box: the client's Mis solicitudes booking date uses the same `CalendarClock text-[#9ca3af]` row (replacing the old `📅` emoji), and project cards show a **"Publicado el {date}"** row with a gray `CalendarDays` (projects have no cita date; the publish date is the equivalent). Don't wrap a date in a coloured container, and keep the date icon the SAME gray as the card's other icons. **(3) meta rows** — `MapPin`/`FileText` icons (`text-[#9ca3af]`) + service `(profesión · ubicación, location muted)` and the client note, sharing the appointment block and separated from the featured date by **spacing only** (`gap-2.5`, no hairline — sprint 362; the bold date vs. lighter meta already reads as a hierarchy). **(4-5) THE PEOPLE — a LEFT/RIGHT split (sprint 359).** Below the appointment, a third-party booking shows the two people SIDE-BY-SIDE with an **aesthetic divider** that adapts: `<div className="border-t border-[#f3f4f6] sm:border-t-0 sm:border-l">` — a horizontal line when the columns STACK on mobile, a vertical line between them on `sm+`. The columns are `flex-col sm:flex-row`, each `flex-1 min-w-0 px-[18px] py-4`. **LEFT = "LA CITA ES PARA"** (eyebrow `text-[#0089bb]`) — the PATIENT (a health dependent): an `h-10` brand-tint initials avatar (`bg-[#EBF5FB] text-[#009FD9]`, beneficiary has no account photo) + name `text-[15px] font-bold` + age pill + **only the birth date** (`nacimiento`, when present). Since sprint 366 we collect ONLY the beneficiary's name + DOB, so this column shows **no cédula, no phone, no WhatsApp** — the requester is the sole contact. **RIGHT = "RESERVADO POR"** (eyebrow `text-[#9ca3af]`) — the REQUESTER / coordination contact: an `h-10` photo avatar (grey-`#f3f4f6` fallback) + name + `tel (ALWAYS — required) · cédula` + their **"WhatsApp a {nombre}"** pill (always). The appointment is NOT inside either column — it's the shared full-width block ABOVE, so the columns stay balanced. **SELF booking → NO split:** one person (`h-11` brand-tint avatar + name + age pill + `tel · cédula · nacimiento` + their WhatsApp), no eyebrows, no "para" side. Each person's WhatsApp lives in THEIR column, labeled by first name so the pro knows who is who; NOT in the footer. **(6) actions** (`py-3.5`, `border-t`): **Confirmar** (blue pill `rounded-full px-6` + `Check` icon) + **Cancelar** (outline pill), **inline-wrap** (the prototype keeps them inline, not full-width-stacked — this card overrides the generic stacked rule above) + a `self-start` "Reportar cliente" flag link below — **no WhatsApp button in the footer**. **Tokens** mirror the app's brand hex (`#009FD9`/`#EBF5FB`/`#bfdbfe`/`#0089bb`, amber via `Badge variant="warning"`, WhatsApp `#25d366`) — within a hair of the prototype's `#008ce0`/`#e8f4fc`/`#b6def7`/`#036aa8` but kept app-consistent. Age markers (**Menor de edad / Adulto mayor**) use the shared **`Badge variant="warning"`** (amber, `text-[11px] font-semibold`) — the SAME treatment as every status badge, NOT a one-off smaller pill (sprint 499); rendered by the single `ageBadge()` helper for both the self-patient and a beneficiary. DOBs use `formatDobDMY` (dd/mm/yyyy). **Badge rule: every status/flag chip goes through the `Badge` component** (variants `default`/`success`/`warning`/`error`/`muted`) — never hand-roll a `rounded-full` pill, so the whole app's badges stay one cohesive system. **Expanded details order (sprint 501):** the **"Solicitada el {date}"** request timestamp is a quiet `Clock`-led meta line at the **TOP** of the expanded panel (NOT buried in the contact card's footer — it's secondary "when it came in" meta, distinct from the prominent appointment date); the service + location + phone/cédula form ONE grey `divide-y` details card; and the **client's NOTE stays its OWN separate field** — a white card with a brand left-accent + "NOTA DEL CLIENTE" eyebrow, visually distinct from the grey details so it clearly reads as their message (never merged into the structured info block).

### Panel section cards — the cohesive "elegant + spacious" spec (sprint 517)
- **The four manage-sections (Verificación · Oportunidades · Solicitudes recibidas · Mis publicaciones — and, sharing the component, Mis solicitudes) follow ONE spacing/hierarchy spec so they read as a cohesive, premium family.** The owner's elegance comes from SPECIFIC, repeatable techniques — NOT "make it nicer". When editing these (or adding a new manage-section card), match this spec:
  - **Whitespace (generous, consistent):** collapsed list cards `p-4 sm:p-5`; expanded bodies `px-4 pb-5 pt-4 sm:px-5`; intra-body gap `gap-3.5`; between cards `gap-3.5`; grouped inner blocks (details card rows, budget/timeline cells, the "para otra persona" callout, the NOTA card) `px-4 py-3.5`/`px-4 py-3`. The Oportunidades **detail pane** is roomier still (`p-5 sm:p-6`, body `gap-4`). Verificación's status **Banner** + its cédula/appeal cards are `p-5 sm:p-6`.
  - **Hierarchy (ONE focal point):** the focal line (client/pro name, project title, service) = **`text-[15px] font-bold` navy `#162543`** (uniform across all four — don't drop to `text-[14px]`/`semibold`); secondary meta = `text-[13px] text-[#6b7280]`; tertiary (relative time, eyebrows, "enviada el") = `text-[11px] text-[#9ca3af]`. Put a `mt-1` between the focal line and the first meta row so the focal stands alone.
  - **Alignment:** lead avatars are **`h-11 w-11`** with a **`gap-3.5`** to the content, so every card shares one left grid + gutter.
  - **Restrained color:** navy focal · blue `#0089bb`/`#009FD9` ONLY for price/key facts + the one help/learn-more link · light greys (`#f3f4f6`/`#f9fafb`/`#EBF5FB`) for fills/dividers. Color is the accent, not the field.
  - **Refined cards:** keep the base `<Card>` (`rounded-2xl border border-[#e5e7eb] shadow-sm`) and add a soft **`hover:shadow-md`** lift; the eyebrow micro-labels stay `text-[10px] font-bold uppercase tracking-[0.06em] text-[#9ca3af]`.
  - **Minimal:** elegance comes from spacing/weight/alignment — do NOT add new elements, icons, or text to achieve it. **The FILTERS are liked and stay byte-for-byte** (`StatusFilterTabs` underline + counts, the Oportunidades browse/mine sub-nav) — redesign everything EXCEPT the filtering.

### Long user text in cards = `<ExpandableText>` (sprint 521) + titles WRAP, never hard-cut
- **User-entered, char-limited text (descriptions, notes, proposal messages — 300/500 chars) renders via the shared `<ExpandableText text lines={N} />`** (`components/ui/expandable-text.tsx`): it clamps to `N` lines (`-webkit-line-clamp`) and shows a **"Ver más / Ver menos"** toggle ONLY when the content actually overflows (measured `scrollHeight > clientHeight` while clamped; the flag stays true so "Ver menos" persists). Always wraps safely (`whitespace-pre-line` + `[overflow-wrap:anywhere]`) so long unbroken strings never overflow. Keeps cards compact while making everything fully readable. Applied to: **Mis publicaciones** (project description + each proposal's message — the message used to be a dead `line-clamp-2`), **Mis solicitudes** (service description), **Solicitudes recibidas** (the client NOTE), **Oportunidades** detail (project description), **Mis propuestas** (the proposal message). i18n: `common.seeMore` / `common.seeLess`. Use this for ANY long panel text — do NOT leave a bare `line-clamp` with no way to expand, and do NOT dump unbounded text that bloats the card.
- **Card TITLES wrap to 2 lines, they don't hard-cut.** Collapsed-card titles/names (client name, project title, proposal's project title) use **`min-w-0 flex-1 line-clamp-2 [overflow-wrap:anywhere]`** (NOT `truncate` — a single-line clip read as an ugly hard cut with many characters) in a `flex items-start justify-between gap-2.5` row, with the status badge **`shrink-0`**. The title's **`flex-1`** is what guarantees the badge can NEVER overlap/cover the title (sprint 523 — the Mis propuestas "Pendiente" badge was colliding with the title because the title had no `flex-1` to reserve its track): the title fills the row, the badge sits in its own fixed-width column with the gap between. A clean 2-line clamp — never a mid-word chop, never an overlap.
- **Per-card actions: primary LEFT + overflow menu RIGHT (consistent, sprint 523).** The expanded-card action row is `flex items-center gap-2` with the **primary button (when any) on the LEFT** and the **`CardActionsMenu` ("···" mobile / "Acciones" desktop) ALWAYS `ml-auto` (right-aligned)** — never `className={primary ? "" : "ml-auto"}` (which left the menu floating mid-row next to the primary, reading as poorly placed). Applied to Mis publicaciones + Mis solicitudes; keep this primary-left / overflow-right shape across all sections so they look intentional + uniform.

### Booking success → lead to the request + refresh /buscar (sprint 524)
- **The booking success screen is NOT a dead-end "Listo".** Its footer leads with a **primary "Ver mi solicitud"** (`success.viewRequest`) that closes the modal and `router.push`es **`/dashboard/profesional?tab=sent_bookings`** — the just-created request sits at the TOP of Mis solicitudes (newest first) — over a secondary "Listo" (`success.close`). The **WhatsApp** fast-contact stays in the body (connector model).
- **Closing the success dialog REFRESHES the page** so the booked slot disappears from /buscar (and the profile schedule). The modal sets `bookedRef` on a successful POST; `resetAndClose()` (called by the "Listo" button, "Ver mi solicitud", the X, Esc, and scrim — all via `onOpenChange`) calls **`router.refresh()`** when `bookedRef` is set, so the server re-reads availability and the now-occupied slot is gone. Use `router.refresh()` (not a manual refetch) for "reflect the new server state after a mutation" on server-rendered pages.

### Oportunidades + Solicitudes recibidas — clean, scannable cards (sprint 529)
- **Oportunidades list card = JOB-focused, not person-focused.** The card leads with the **TITLE (focal: `text-[15px] font-bold` navy, `line-clamp-2`)** + a "Nueva" badge, then the **BUDGET prominent right under it** (`text-[15px] font-bold text-[#0089bb]` — the number the pro decides on), then a **quiet meta row** (zona · plazo · relative time, `text-[12px] text-[#6b7280]` with small `#9ca3af` `MapPin`/`CalendarClock`/`Clock` icons), and the **CLIENT as a tertiary footer** (a small `h-6` avatar + name, separated by a hairline). The OLD card led with a big client avatar (read as "a person", not "a job to bid on"). The desktop master-detail (list + sticky detail pane) + the mobile accordion + the propuesta form are unchanged; only the list card's hierarchy was rebuilt. Vertical `flex-col gap-2.5 p-4 sm:p-5`.
- **Solicitudes recibidas stays person-focused (its correct focal IS "who"), and is COHESIVE with the above:** collapsed = avatar + **client name (focal)** + status + the appointment date (`CalendarClock`) + the **service** as a clean `Wrench`-led meta line (`getCategoryLabel(category_id)` — replaced the old mixed "servicio · nota" truncated snippet; this now mirrors the client's Mis solicitudes card 1:1). Same card shell, type scale (15px bold navy focal / 12-13px grey meta / small dark icons), `hover:shadow-md`, and restrained color (blue only for the key number) as Oportunidades — so the two read as one family. The expanded Solicitudes layout (para-otra-persona callout · grey details card · NOTA card · actions) is unchanged.

### Panel date labels = relative time, then the date past ~1 week (sprint 528)
- **Every PAST-event date label across the panels uses `formatRelativeOrDate(date, locale)` (`lib/utils`):** escalates **"hace un momento" → "hace X minutos" → "hace X horas" → "hace X días"**, then shows the **actual date** ("22 jun 2026") once it's **~1 week or older** (never "hace N semanas/meses"). Keep the **context prefix** per spot: **Oportunidades** post time (`postedAgo` + the list card's `· {relative}`; the "Nueva" today-badge stays), **Solicitudes recibidas** "Solicitada {x}", **Mis publicaciones** "Publicado {x}", **Mis propuestas** "Enviada {x}", **Notificaciones** (each row's time). The prefix keys dropped "el"/"on" so they read "Solicitada hace 2 horas" / "Solicitada 22 jun 2026". **Only for PAST events** (created/sent/published) — a FUTURE appointment date (Mis solicitudes cita, the schedule) stays an absolute date/time. `formatRelativeTime` (the older weeks/months variant) is superseded for panel labels by `formatRelativeOrDate`.

### Mis solicitudes (client) card content (sprint 522)
- **A client request card shows WHO + WHAT + FOR WHOM** — symmetric with the pro's Solicitudes recibidas. **Collapsed:** pro name (the toggle) + status + appointment date (`CalendarClock`) + **the service** (`Wrench` + `getCategoryLabel(booking.category_id ?? professionals.category_id)` — NOT `professionals.categories.name`, which the bookings API does NOT return: there's no FK join, so resolve the label from the booking's own `category_id`) + a quiet **"Para otra persona"** hint (`Users`) when `for_someone_else`. **Expanded:** a grouped block = the **professional as a clickable row → `/profesionales/{slug}`** (avatar + "PROFESIONAL" eyebrow + name + `ExternalLink`; the clickable name lives HERE, not in the collapsed header, which stays the expand-toggle) + a **"SERVICIO"** row; then, for a third-party booking, the **"La cita es para" brand-tint callout** = beneficiary name + age + the **"Menor de edad"/"Adulto mayor" `Badge variant="warning"`** (`ageCategoryFromDob`), identical to the pro side; then the client's note (`<ExpandableText>`) + the actions. The client booking type carries `category_id`/`for_someone_else`/`beneficiary_*` (the API already returns them via `*`).

### Mobile menu (left drawer) — grouped sections, both auth states
- **Always grouped under uppercase section headers** (`text-[10px] font-bold text-gray-400 uppercase tracking-widest`), never a flat list. Keep the left slide-in drawer + swipe-to-close + scrim.
- **Logged-in order:** search → **MI CUENTA** (avatar + name/email header, then Mi panel [bold + brand icon], Mis solicitudes, Mis proyectos, Mis favoritos, Ofrecer mis servicios [client-only], Cuenta y seguridad — all **role-aware**, with icons) → CATEGORÍAS → RECURSOS → Idioma ES/EN → **Cerrar sesión** (red, bottom). Surface the account area **right after the search** so the panel is one tap away.
- **Logged-out order:** search → **CATEGORÍAS** (+ "Ver todas") → **CUENTA** (Ingresar [outline] + Registrarse como profesional [brand pill]) → RECURSOS → Idioma ES/EN. Rationale: clients **register at booking time, not upfront**, so there is **no "register as client"** option — a new client only needs to **search/browse**, then requesting a service creates their account. The two account actions are surfaced **right after browse** (clearly visible, but not first since clients won't register upfront); Recursos + Idioma are utility, last. No "Mi cuenta" area when logged out.
- **Categorías — full grouped dropdown on DESKTOP, single link on MOBILE (sprint 525).** The desktop "Categorías" mega-menu's default view is built from the **FULL catalog `CATEGORY_GROUPS`** (all 12 groups, each a section with its `getCategoryGroupLabel` header + all its `/buscar?categoria=<id>` categories) in a **3-column flow** (`[column-count:3]` + `break-inside-avoid`, `max-h-[64vh] overflow-y-auto`, panel `w-[720px]`) — NOT a hand-curated short list; typing still **filters the list in place** + the "¿No ves tu categoría?" suggest + "Ver todas las categorías"→/categorias footer stay. **On mobile the hamburger drawer shows ONE "Categorías" link → /categorias** (no inline category list) — the full grouped browse lives on the categorías page. Don't re-add a curated `CATEGORY_COLUMNS` subset or an inline mobile category list.
- **Notifications are reachable from the menu, not just the bell:** include a role-aware **"Notificaciones"** item (with the unread badge) in the desktop account dropdown AND the mobile MI CUENTA section (`tab=notifications`, role-aware href). The expanded desktop header also keeps the standalone bell. **ONE notification icon everywhere = the `Bell` (sprint 500):** the panel-nav "Notificaciones" item, the navbar bell, AND every row of the Notificaciones list use the SAME `Bell` (list rows: a `Bell` in a neutral `bg-[#f3f4f6]` circle with a brand-blue unread corner-dot) — NOT per-type icons (the kind is clear from the title; one shared glyph reads consistently as "your notifications").
- **NO filter on the Notificaciones list (sprint 516).** The old **"Todas / No leídas"** `StatusFilterTabs` was REMOVED — it added a tab row of clutter for no real value: unread is already conveyed by the row highlight (`bg-[#f3f9fd]`) + the brand-blue corner-dot + the **"Marcar todas como leídas"** action, the list is **mode-scoped** (only the active mode's ~3-4 notification types) + capped + usually short, and each title makes its type obvious. A **type filter was considered and rejected** for the same reason (too few, too heterogeneous to be worth a filter). The header is now just the right-aligned actions (**Marcar todas como leídas** when there's unread · **Eliminar todas**). Rule going forward: **no filter is better than a useless one** — only add a filter when the list is genuinely long AND the filter dimension is something the user can't already see at a glance.
